- **dk-experiment-agent**: Use this agent for any question a feature's plan rests on that only running code can answer. It measures across the whole set when that is free, saves what it measured, and reports every answer with how many records it was checked on. Spawn one per approved experiment, several at once. Brief each with `plan=&lt;path&gt; experiment=E&lt;n&gt;`, because it sees none of your conversation:… ([dezmon-fernandez/skillz](https://github.com/dezmon-fernandez/skillz/blob/HEAD/agents/dk-experiment-agent.md))
- **dk-external-research-agent**: Use this agent to find what a coordinator must read from outside the codebase before planning a feature. It researches one topic (a library, an external API or service, a standard or protocol, or a technique) and reports references, each a section-anchored link or a local document path with the exact thing it documents and why, plus pitfalls and breaking changes. Spawn several at once, one per topic. Brief each with `plan=&lt;path&gt; topic=T&lt;n&gt;`, because it sees none of your conversation: the plan's numbered topic holds what the feature needs from it, the version in use, where the code uses it now… ([dezmon-fernandez/skillz](https://github.com/dezmon-fernandez/skillz/blob/HEAD/agents/dk-external-research-agent.md))
- **dk-git**: Orquestrador da etapa de git do DK — configurar o local, achar o projeto no GitLab, commitar e abrir merge request, sempre atrás das guardas. ([odoisbr/dk](https://github.com/odoisbr/dk/blob/HEAD/agents/dk-git.md))

- **dk-levantar**: Orquestrador da etapa de levantamento do DK — insumo de reunião até requisitos rastreáveis. ([odoisbr/dk](https://github.com/odoisbr/dk/blob/HEAD/agents/dk-levantar.md))
- **dk-prototipar**: Orquestrador da etapa de protótipo do DK — mudança com alvo declarado, dentro do padrão. ([odoisbr/dk](https://github.com/odoisbr/dk/blob/HEAD/agents/dk-prototipar.md))

- **dm-reviewer**: Delivery-machine reviewer. Reviews one builder branch for correctness bugs and needless complexity. Spawned by /dm after a builder finishes. ([badman-returns/janus](https://github.com/badman-returns/janus/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/dm-reviewer.md))
- **dm-revisor-codigo**: Revisor estricto de código Python del proyecto (src/, app/, tests/, scripts): corrección, fallos silenciosos en pipelines de datos, convenciones del repo, tipos, tests y simplicidad. Úsalo de forma proactiva tras cambiar código y antes de abrir su PR. Solo revisa; da un veredicto (APTO, APTO CON RESERVAS o NO APTO) con evidencia. ([Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io](https://github.com/Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dm-revisor-codigo.md) · 2 copies)
- **dm-revisor-diseno**: Revisor de solo lectura del diseño de lo que ve el público (app/, informe, páginas y diapositivas del pitch): accesibilidad, suelo visual, convenciones web y móvil, señales de interfaz generada, estados y textos de interfaz, sobre el render y con cifras. Úsalo de forma proactiva tras cambiar el aspecto de una pantalla o diapositiva y antes de una demo. Solo revisa; da un veredicto. ([Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io](https://github.com/Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dm-revisor-diseno.md) · 2 copies)
- **dm-revisor-espacial**: Revisor estricto de trabajo espacial (cartografía y uniones, W y exploración, modelos, validación, intervalos, interpretación y mapas). Úsalo de forma proactiva tras cambiar código o resultados territoriales y antes de presentar una cifra, un mapa o un modelo espacial o abrir su PR. Solo revisa; da un veredicto (APTO, APTO CON RESERVAS o NO APTO) con evidencia. ([Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io](https://github.com/Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dm-revisor-espacial.md) · 2 copies)
- **dm-revisor-seguridad**: Revisor de seguridad de solo lectura: secretos, entradas no confiables (APIs, ficheros, usuarios de la demo), inyección, SSRF, deserialización, HTML inseguro en app/, prompt injection en asistentes con LLM y dependencias. Úsalo de forma proactiva tras cambiar descargas, la demo, un MCP o dependencias y antes de publicar. Da un veredicto con evidencia. ([Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io](https://github.com/Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dm-revisor-seguridad.md) · 2 copies)
- **dm-trazador-cifras**: Rastrea cada cifra que ve el público (app, guion del pitch, respuestas del asistente) hasta el código, la consulta y el dato que la producen, y comprueba que lleva fuente y la marca de estimado si lo es. Úsalo de forma proactiva antes de una demo, un ensayo del pitch o un PR que cambie cifras visibles. Solo lee y recalcula; da un veredicto. ([Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io](https://github.com/Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dm-trazador-cifras.md) · 2 copies)
- **dm-verificador-fuentes**: Verificador de solo lectura de afirmaciones sobre datos, normas y licencias (existencia, cobertura, año, licencia, vigencia, cifras) contra el publicador original. Úsalo de forma proactiva para resolver marcas «(verificar)», antes de añadir una fuente a data/README.md y antes de presentar una cifra citada. Devuelve estado, evidencia y texto corregido; no edita. ([Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io](https://github.com/Kuantoapp/kuantoapp.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dm-verificador-fuentes.md) · 2 copies)
- **dm-verifier**: Delivery-machine verifier. Proves a slice works by running tests, booting the real stack, driving the real UI, and writing proof/&lt;slice&gt;/ with screenshots. Nothing is done until this agent's proof exists. Spawned by /dm after review. ([badman-returns/janus](https://github.com/badman-returns/janus/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/dm-verifier.md))
- **dm-yanitlayici**: Yorum→DM metnini ve DM cevap taslaklarını yazar. ([ahmedsdesigntr/watfe](https://github.com/ahmedsdesigntr/watfe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dm-yanitlayici.md))
- **dm3-expert**: DM3-domänauktoritet. Äger frågan "vad gör en stark människa här, och hur snabbt?" — korpusbaslinjer, ruttdefinitioner, gap-specar, kurerat navinnehåll och item-timing. Använd när en rutt ska definieras, en baslinje hämtas, en mesh-lucka formaliseras, eller ett botbeteende bedömas mot mänskligt spel. ([Xerialen/route-lab](https://github.com/Xerialen/route-lab/blob/HEAD/agents/dm3-expert.md))
- **DM_AGENT**: &gt; **Read this file in full before starting any work.** &gt; **You are the orchestrator. You do NOT write application code. You coordinate agents.** ([ksyed0/PlanVisualizer](https://github.com/ksyed0/PlanVisualizer/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/DM_AGENT.md) · ★6 · 5 copies)
- **dma-assurance-gatekeeper**: Enforces Stage 1 DMA mutability, evidence completeness, and snapshot finalization constraints. ([sankalpsthakur/carbon](https://github.com/sankalpsthakur/carbon/blob/HEAD/plugins/scope3-strategy/agents/dma-assurance-gatekeeper.md))
- **dma-materiality-matrix-assurance-lead**: Governs DMA-first materiality matrix production with stakeholder evidence quality controls and audit-ready traceability. ([sankalpsthakur/carbon](https://github.com/sankalpsthakur/carbon/blob/HEAD/plugins/scope3-strategy/agents/dma-materiality-matrix-assurance-lead.md))
- **dma-physical-frame-allocator**: The kernel heap lives at `KERNEL_VIRT_BASE` (`0xFFFF_FFFF_8000_0000`). The naive `virt_to_phys()` function (`addr - PHYS_MAP_BASE`) returns a bogus ~128TB "physical" address for heap pointers — this is NOT the real physical address the hardware DMAs to. ([chris17453/oxide_os](https://github.com/chris17453/oxide_os/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/dma-physical-frame-allocator.md) · ★3)
- **dma-scanner**: Use this agent when a workflow needs market intelligence across one or more DMAs — demand signals, supply levels, pricing trends, top sellers, and top dealer groups. This agent consolidates all get_sold_summary and search_active_cars calls for DMA-level analysis into a single parallel subprocess. ([MarketcheckHub/marketcheck-cowork-plugin](https://github.com/MarketcheckHub/marketcheck-cowork-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/auction-house/agents/dma-scanner.md) · ★4)
- **dmad-archaeologist**: Reconstitue l'intention derrière les règles d'un legacy à partir de l'historique git, des tickets et des noms de tests. Produit exclusivement des hypothèses à valider par un humain. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-archaeologist.md))
- **dmad-cartographer**: Construit le Knowledge Graph DMAD par traversée depuis les points d'entrée. Trace les appels, les accès aux données et les franchissements de frontière, en journalisant chaque arrêt. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-cartographer.md))
- **dmad-carver**: Découpe un legacy en capacités métier pour DMAD, en croisant cohésion d'appels, partage de données, couplage temporel git et vocabulaire. Prépare le gate humain le plus important de la méthode. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-carver.md))
- **dmad-challenger**: Attaque les affirmations produites par DMAD selon 9 angles pour les faire tomber. Ne rédige jamais de documentation, peut uniquement dégrader une confiance, jamais la promouvoir. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-challenger.md))
- **dmad-curator**: Garantit la cohérence d'un run DMAD - glossaire unifié, contradictions inter-capacités, renvois, rapport de couverture, questions ouvertes priorisées, détection des affirmations périmées. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-curator.md))
- **dmad-diagram-planner**: Décide quels diagrammes produire pour un run DMAD, chacun répondant à une question explicite et respectant un seuil de lisibilité. Peut refuser un diagramme. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-diagram-planner.md))
- **dmad-elucidator**: Reconstitue les cas d'usage et extrait les règles de gestion d'une capacité pour DMAD, chaque règle localisée ligne à ligne et plafonnée au niveau inféré. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-elucidator.md))
- **dmad-scoper**: Cadre un run DMAD de rétro-documentation. Mène l'entretien de cadrage, produit scope.yaml, refuse de démarrer sur un périmètre flou. À utiliser au tout début d'une analyse de legacy. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-scoper.md))
- **dmad-surveyor**: Recense mécaniquement une codebase pour DMAD - stack, points d'entrée, modules, schéma de données, intégrations, tests, hotspots. Produit uniquement des faits, jamais d'interprétation. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-surveyor.md))
- **dmad-test-forger**: Écrit des tests de caractérisation qui prouvent les règles de gestion documentées par DMAD. Seul agent autorisé à promouvoir une affirmation au niveau vérifié. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-test-forger.md))
- **dmad-writer-functional**: Rédige la documentation fonctionnelle d'un run DMAD à partir des seules affirmations validées, sans accès au code source, en vocabulaire métier strict. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-writer-functional.md))
- **dmad-writer-technical**: Rédige la documentation technique d'un run DMAD (arc42/C4, fiches de module, seams) à partir du graphe et des affirmations validées, sans relire le code source. ([jmellano/dmad-method](https://github.com/jmellano/dmad-method/blob/HEAD/plugins/dmad/agents/dmad-writer-technical.md))
- **dmaic-coordinator**: Coordinator para DMAIC — Six Sigma process improvement, 5 fases (Define/Measure/Analyze/Improve/Control) con tollgates formales. Usar cuando la metodología DMAIC está activa. Worktree aislado. ([jcg-admin/IACT-docs](https://github.com/jcg-admin/IACT-docs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dmaic-coordinator.md) · 6 copies)
- **dmaker-apurador**: Levanta fatos do DMaker e devolve condensado: consulta o Jira (projeto DMK) com comentários, mapeia PRs (abertos e mergeados) e o que está na develop, roda portões, lê CHANGELOG e histórico do git, reproduz e mede (render, QA, transcrição). Não muda nada. Use quando o planejamento precisa de dados cujo volume bruto não cabe no contexto de quem decide. ([DougFSA/DMaker](https://github.com/DougFSA/DMaker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dmaker-apurador.md))
- **dmaker-executor**: Executa um plano já decidido num chamado do DMaker (projeto DMK): implementa o código, roda os portões, commita, abre o PR, mergeia o PR na develop e só então move o card para Teste Manual no Jira. Recebe um briefing pronto; não decide escopo nem estratégia. Use quando o planejamento já foi feito e o que falta é o trabalho mecânico de um card. ([DougFSA/DMaker](https://github.com/DougFSA/DMaker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dmaker-executor.md))
- **dmarc-report-reader**: Read a directory of DMARC aggregate reports (rua XML, plain, zipped or gzipped, two to four weeks from many receivers) and return a sender inventory classified as working, fixable, forwarder or list, or unknown, with the rows that decided each class, so the XML never enters the caller's context. Use when a pile of DMARC or rua report files needs reading before anyone decides what to fix. Not for setting up SPF, DKIM or DMARC, repairing a failing selector or stepping the policy (email-authentication-rollout), or inbox placement, bounces and complaint rates (deliverability-triage). ([greenblacked/AI](https://github.com/greenblacked/AI/blob/HEAD/plugins/growth/agents/dmarc-report-reader.md))
- **DMAssistant.agent**: DM help router for Northwatch Wardens using Observe → Plan → Act → Reflect loops for session prep, adventure creation, NPC design, and canon validation ([jahitchcock/Northwatch-Wardens---HomeBrew-](https://github.com/jahitchcock/Northwatch-Wardens---HomeBrew-/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/DMAssistant.agent.md) · ★1)
- **DMDeveloper**: Full-stack development agent for the DungeonMaster D&D 5e rules engine, Fastify game server, and CLI harness. Use for implementing features, refactoring, debugging, and architecture work. ([mweyamutsvene/DungeonMaster](https://github.com/mweyamutsvene/DungeonMaster/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/developer.agent.md))
- **dmf-agent**: Specialist agent for D365 DMF export/import monitoring. Reads all configuration from parsed-schema.json — never hardcodes event names, column names, or thresholds. ([prashantdce21MSFT/d365-observability-hub](https://github.com/prashantdce21MSFT/d365-observability-hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/dmf-agent.md) · ★4)
